- [x] **Notes: paste as plain text (Ctrl+Shift+V); pasted tables now become tables** (2026-10-05).
  - **Paste as plain text** was already half there: ProseMirror pastes as plain text while Shift is held, so Ctrl+Shift+V gave text without the source's formatting or links, and the text takes the formatting where it lands (Word's "Keep text only"). It was undiscoverable, and our own `handlePaste` could undo it: it turned tab-separated or Markdown-table text into a table, and inserted a copied image, whether or not Shift was held.
    - `NoteEditor`'s `handleKeyDown` now records a Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+V in `plainPasteRef`. The next `handlePaste` sees it and steps aside, leaving ProseMirror's plain-text paste alone.
    - Registered as `notes-paste-plain` in `hotkeys.ts`, so it shows in Settings.
  - **Bug found and fixed: pasting a table never made a table.** `insertTableFromData` looked up `table_row` / `table_header` / `table_cell`, ProseMirror's default node names, but Tiptap's are `tableRow` / `tableHeader` / `tableCell`. The lookup was always empty, so it returned false and the paste fell through as plain text. This has been the case since the feature was added (`e667323`).
  - **Tests** (`NoteEditor.test.tsx`, on the real editor):
    - A normal paste keeps bold and links.
    - Ctrl+Shift+V drops them.
    - Tab-separated text becomes a table on a normal paste, and stays text after Ctrl+Shift+V.
  - **Since built (2026-10-06):** Paste as plain text is also in the editor's right-click menu (see the right-click entry below). **Not verified:** Android (the hotkey's touch path names the system's own Paste as plain text, which depends on the keyboard).
- [x] **Right-click menus (suite-wide); Notes editor, notebook and note right-click menus; scroll past the end of a note** (2026-10-06).
  - **Right-click menus.** Asked for after "paste as plain text", with the brief that what a right-click shows is defined by where it lands (app, view, element) and that flexibility and future-proofing come first. The pattern, model and extension points are in CLAUDE.md "Right-click menus".
    - **Files:** `src/contextMenu/{types,registry,useContextMenuScope}.ts`; `components/ContextMenu/{ContextMenuHost,ContextMenu}.tsx` (+ CSS); `components/NoteEditor/contextMenu.ts`; `components/RowHoverActions/rowActionItems.ts`.
    - **Consumers:**
      - `RowOptionsMenu` declares a `row` scope from its `RowAction` children: all 7 row sites, nothing per site.
      - `NoteList` rows declare `note-row`.
      - `NoteEditor` declares `note-editor` with `NoteEditorMenuApi` (paste, openLink, openCreate, insertTitle).
    - Labels in `LABELS.contextMenu` (+ `LABELS.rowActions.indentNote`, now also the inline ↳'s title).
  - **Decisions:**
    - Shift+right-click keeps the browser's own menu (spellcheck), as agreed with the user.
    - Unclaimed places and text inputs keep it too, so nothing regresses where we have nothing to add.
    - Android is untouched: long-press already does this job.
    - The menu never takes focus, so the editor's selection survives for Cut/Copy.
    - Scopes stop at the innermost one that contributes, by default: right-click a row → the row's menu, not the section's too. `propagate: true` opts in to more.
    - Rejected:
      - A DOM-attribute registry. Scopes need live data and callbacks, so a WeakMap from element to a "describe me" function is used instead.
      - Firing synthetic keystrokes to reach existing hotkey actions. `FloatingToolbar`'s Ctrl+L / Ctrl+Q logic became `openLinkInput` / `openCreateMenu`, which both the hotkeys and the menu call (passed up via `actionsRef`). `NoteEditor`'s no-selection link pane is `openNewLinkPane`. The toolbar's 🔗 button lost its duplicate `openLinkInput`.
  - **Menu Paste** reads `navigator.clipboard`, which needs permission: a browser may prompt, and a refusal shows an alert pointing at Ctrl+V / Ctrl+Shift+V. It goes through ProseMirror's `pasteHTML` / `pasteText`, so it behaves like the keys: an image is shrunk and inserted, table-shaped text becomes a table, and plain is plain.
  - **Scroll past the end of a note.** (Since 2026-10-08 a real element, `NoteBackdrop.tsx`, with a choosable background: see "Notes and navigation round, 2026-10-08".) `.editorWrap::after`, `flex: 0 0 80%` of the scroller's height, adds that much room below the text. It is filled with the new `--color-overscroll` token (light #e8e9ed, dark #2e2e33, under a `--color-border-strong` line) so it reads as outside the page. Purpose: the last lines can sit mid-screen, clear of the + button.
  - **Tests:**
    - `contextMenu/registry.test.ts`: scope order, innermost wins, skip-empty, propagate, provider order/data/when, the implicit section scope, HMR replace/unregister.
    - `components/ContextMenu/ContextMenuHost.test.tsx`: shows instead of the browser menu; leaves it for undeclared places, Shift and inputs; run/close, disabled; keyboard incl. submenu → / Escape; outside press; a `RowOptionsMenu` row's actions, skipping a conditional `false` child.
    - Two pattern tests: no other `contextmenu` listener, and the host is mounted once.
  - **Verified:**
    - Full Vitest suite (944), `tsc -b`, `npm run build`; eslint clean on the new files. `NoteEditor` has the same 8 baseline `react-hooks` errors as before, at shifted lines.
    - Headless Chromium on the dev server, clipboard permission granted:
      - The editor menu lists its 8 items in 4 sections.
      - Copy put the selection on the clipboard with the editor still focused.
      - Paste as plain text inserted text without bold; Paste kept bold.
      - Style ▸ Heading 2 applied.
      - Shift+right-click showed no custom menu.
      - The notebook row showed Add section / Edit / Delete; the note row showed Open / Edit details / Delete.
      - The overscroll room measured 534px of a 668px view (80%), screenshots in light and dark.
  - **Not verified:** the Tauri desktop app's clipboard permission for menu Paste (WebView2); Android (unchanged by design).
- [x] **Restore marker: a backup restore wins on every signed-in device** (2026-10-06). The 2026-10-05 fix made a restore upload the restored data for every synced store, but the restored records keep their old `updatedAt`. Another device holding newer copies would then push them back on its next load (`queueLocalOnlyAndNewer`), undoing the restore.
  - **Decision (user, 2026-10-06):** one account-level marker, not a per-record "restored" field (that would need a column on ~17 tables plus every type, mapper and store migration), and not restamping `updatedAt` to the restore time (cheaper, but "last edited" dates would change).
  - **How it works:**
    - `restoreBackupData` → `forceUpload` → `markRestored(userId)`, which upserts `sync_markers.restored_at` (migration `042`, new table) and records the marker as seen on this device.
    - `runInitSync` reads the marker alongside the tables. If it's newer than the one this device last saw (`todo-sync-restore-seen`), it sets `restoreCutoff` for that one hydrate:
      - `dropPendingBefore` drops queued changes older than the restore, including undatable queued deletes;
      - `mergeRecords` gives older local copies the cloud's version and drops older local-only records.
    - Records edited after the restore keep the normal newest-wins rule.
    - The marker is recorded as seen only once every table loaded.
    - A device with no seen record (never loaded the account) only records it, so guest data being brought into the account is kept.
    - A table that can't be read (migration not run) means the marker is ignored.
  - **Files:** `src/services/sync/syncService.ts` ("Restore marker" section, `MARKER_TABLE`, deliberately not in `SYNC_TABLES`), `src/utils/backupExport.ts`, `supabase/migrations/042_sync_markers.sql`, CLAUDE.md (How sync works, migration tables, table summary). Planned notifications migrations renumbered to `043` (done dates) and `044` (tracker reminders) in `docs/android/05-notifications.md` and BACKLOG.md.
  - **Known limits:**
    - A device that loads in the few seconds between the restore's upload and its marker write can still push old copies first.
    - Records without `updatedAt` (tags, list types, portfolio tags/purposes) already merge remote-wins, so they follow the restore anyway.
    - A pending delete made on another device after the restore but before its next load is dropped (queued deletes carry no time).
  - **Tests:** 6 new cases in `src/services/sync/syncService.test.ts` (an unseen restore applies; it applies only once; a new device only records it; queued pre-restore changes are dropped; an unreadable table changes nothing; `markRestored` doesn't re-apply to itself). **Verified:** `tsc -b`, full Vitest suite. **Not verified:** against the live database (migration `042` pending) or across two real devices.
- [x] **Notifications rebuilt on one set of rules; Android system notifications; "done" on Reminders/Deadlines** (2026-10-06). Workstream W9, design signed off 2026-10-04 in `docs/android/05-notifications.md`; that doc's "Build log" is the detailed record.
  - **Why:** reminders are one of the main reasons to have the app on a phone (D9), and the desktop checker had two live bugs.
    - **No Deadline notified anywhere** from 2026-09-27 (the Deadline kind) until this change: the checker had no deadlines loop, and task shadows had moved to Deadlines.
    - **Repeating items notified only on their first date.**
  - **Shared rules:** `services/notifications/plan.ts` `planNotifications(snapshot, {from, to, zone, clockFormat})`, pure.
    - Events: notify-before.
    - Reminders: at their time, or whole-day at `notifyDaysBefore`/`notifyAtTime`.
    - Deadlines: always before.
    - Committed Schedule blocks: 30 minutes before.
    - A snooze (`remindAt`) adds one notification and supersedes earlier occurrences.
    - Skips: archived items, task-linked ones whose task is done, and `doneDates`.
    - Every repeating occurrence is expanded via `recurrence.ts`.
    - `hooks/useNotificationChecker.ts` now just fires what the plan says is due (bell + OS on desktop; bell only on Android). `notificationStore` v2 keys the log per occurrence, honouring old item-keyed entries.
  - **Android:** `services/notifications/androidScheduler.ts`, mounted in `App.tsx`. See CLAUDE.md "Notifications — one set of rules" and `05-notifications.md` for channels, buttons, reconcile, permission and quiet hours.
  - **Shared actions:** `services/notifications/actions.ts` (snooze presets `snoozeTime`, `snoozeNotificationTarget`, `markOccurrenceDone`, `completeNotificationTask`, `openNotificationTarget`, `clearNotificationCard`). The bell (`NotificationCenter`) now handles Deadlines and uses `taskId` from the plan.
  - **Done per occurrence:** `doneDates` on `CalendarReminder`/`CalendarDeadline`, `calendarStore` v15, `setOccurrenceDone`, mappers, migration `043_done_dates.sql` (pending). Strikethrough via `isCompletedItem` in `CalendarView`. `ItemActionFooter` gained `completeLabels` for "Mark done / Not done" in the Reminder and Deadline panes.
  - **Settings:** `settingsStore` v6 (`notifyReminders`, `notifyEvents`, `snoozeMorningTime`, `snoozeEveningTime`, `quietHours`); `SettingsPane/NotificationsSection.tsx` (Android only); `Toggle`/`SettingRow` moved to `SettingsPane/SettingControls.tsx`. The Escape pattern test's pinned SettingsPane line moved 110 → 84 as a result.
  - **Pattern:** CLAUDE.md "Notifications — one set of rules", with a pattern test (delivery only from the checker and the scheduler).
  - **Not built:** tracker/routine "Remind me" (N7, with Records, on hold); Done in a calendar item's long-press sheet (W3).
  - **Verified:** `tsc -b`; full Vitest suite. **Not verified:** anything on the emulator or a phone; desktop notifications weren't exercised by hand either.
- [x] **Inline objects in notes (`\`), and linked text drawn as one object (a pane that expands)** (2026-10-06, revised twice the same day after the user tested it). The user asked for "type `\` to create an object" in Notes, starting with Reminders and extended to almost every other kind soon, so the pattern had to be robust and cheap to extend. They also asked to fix linked text showing its icon on every line, and for a basic/expanded view of a link. The design was agreed in conversation; the user accepted every recommendation (natural language first, Tab/Enter rules, Undo toast, live expanded view, status styling, Android path). The rule for new code is CLAUDE.md "Inline objects in notes (`\`)".
  - **Creating with `\`:**
    - Type `\` at the start of a line or after a space. The `\…` text turns into a draft (tinted, outlined) and a menu opens under it, or above it near the bottom of the screen.
    - Typing narrows the kinds. Tab or Enter picks the highlighted one, so `\rem` Tab becomes `\reminder ` (Enter only once something is typed, or the arrows were used). A unique prefix followed by a space counts as chosen.
    - Then type what it is: `call mum tomorrow 5pm`. The menu becomes a live preview (What / Date / Time), read by the same parser as Ctrl+Q (`inferCalendarItemFromSelection`).
    - **Enter** creates it silently and removes `\reminder `.
      - **What was typed is replaced by the item's title, linked**: the date, time and cues live in the item now.
      - The cursor carries on as plain text: the commit clears the link from the stored marks after its last step, because a step clears stored marks.
      - A toast offers Open and Undo. Undo deletes the item outright (also from the Recycling Bin) and puts the originally typed words back.
    - **Ctrl+Enter / All options** opens the full Add Calendar Item pane prefilled. The text becomes the title and waits as `uiStore.pendingArtifactLink` with `replaceWithTitle`. On creation (`applyResolvedArtifactLink`) it becomes the pane's *final* title, linked, even when nothing was typed (the bug the user reported).
    - **Tab** moves into the preview's fields. They take natural language: "fri" and "next sat" via `whenInput.ts` (see below), "2:30pm". A field it can't read is outlined red and stops Enter with a reason. Esc, or Tab past the last field, returns to the text.
    - **Esc** (or a click elsewhere, or moving the cursor out) ends it and leaves the text as typed. `\\` types one literal backslash.
    - It never triggers inside a word (`C:\Users`), in code, or from paste.
  - **Linked text, for every link (Ctrl+Q's too), drawn as one pane** (the user's design, second round):
    - Pieces of one link are grouped back together, because ProseMirror splits a mark per paragraph and around bold. Each link is drawn once.
    - **Inline:** `[⏰ REMINDER] Call mum [Tomorrow 17:00 · ❗ ✏️ 🔁 · state · ☐ ↗ ▾]`, sized to its contents.
      - It's a head widget, the text's inline decorations and a tail widget sharing one outline.
      - **Clicks:**
        - the title is the note's own text, so a click edits it (it no longer opens the item);
        - the date and the time each become a small input (Enter or leaving saves, Esc cancels; an unreadable value stays open, outlined);
        - ☐ marks done; ↗ opens;
        - **anything else on it expands or collapses.**
      - State: done struck through, overdue's date in the warning colour, deleted greyed with "Deleted" and ✕ unlink. A whole-day item counts as overdue only after its day.
    - **Expanded: the same pane, grown.** The paragraph holding the link becomes the box (a node decoration), with that line as its heading. The body is a widget at the end of the same paragraph, displayed as a block under the heading line.
      - The body holds only what the heading doesn't: the notes as plain text, links as chips (click opens, × removes), the Endeavour as a chip in its colour. No field labels. Each is click-to-edit, and missing ones show as faint "+ Notes / + Link / + Endeavour" hints.
      - Rendered by `ArtifactBody` → the type's `Body` (`CalendarItemBody` → the shared `ObjectBody`) in its own React root.
    - **Right-click on a link:** Open, Show all details / Hide details, Mark done, **Important / Tentative / Repeat ▸** (the type's `menu`; the visual pane doesn't offer the flags), Unlink.
    - The view is stored on the mark (`display: 'basic' | 'expanded'`; default basic, so old notes load unchanged). Live state is redrawn on store changes and every minute.
    - **The mark is inclusive** (typing at the end of a title extends it; the tail widget is `side: 1`, so the cursor there sits before the date).
      - An `appendTransaction` in `ArtifactLinkGroups` drops the link from the stored marks whenever the cursor's line doesn't hold it, so Enter at the end of a title starts a plain line.
      - Home/End on an expanded pane's line are handled by `paneLineKeys`: the body box inside the paragraph made the browser's End jump into the next paragraph.
  - **Third round (same day, the user's feedback):**
    - **Default when nothing is said about when:** a `\reminder` is tomorrow at 12:00; a `\event` is tomorrow 12:00–13:00 (the user's call). A date alone is still a whole day; a time alone is today. Constants: `DEFAULT_OBJECT_TIME` and `defaultObjectDate` in `whenInput.ts`.
    - **Options that are off are offered in the expanded body, greyed** (icon in greyscale too, so it can't read as "on"): Important, Tentative, Repeat (asks how often). A click turns one on, and from then it shows in the heading.
      - **One list per kind** (`flags(id)` → `ArtifactFlag[]`, `objects/flags.ts`: `calendarFlags`) feeds both the body and the right-click menu (`flagMenuItems`). The earlier per-type `menu` is gone.
    - **"Notify me":**
      - Events: opt-in. Greyed "🔔 Notify me" turns it on (1 hour before, the modal's default); the chip is then click-to-change (number, unit, Off).
      - Deadlines (and whole-day Reminders): the calendar *always* notifies these, so instead of an off state the body shows the setting as a chip ("🔔 1 day before, 17:00"), click to change via `AllDayNotifyField`. Told to the user.
      - Files: `objects/NotifyChips.tsx` (`LeadNotifyChip`, `BeforeNotifyChip`).
    - **The done checkbox moved into the icon slot**, shown while the pane is hovered (and kept, ticked, once done). The heading's end no longer has it. It's the row options pattern (an icon that becomes its control on hover): nothing moves, nothing is covered.
      - Hover is tracked per link by the groups plugin (`mouseover`/`mouseleave` → `data-hover` on the head), since head, text and tail are separate elements.
      - Consequence: while hovered, a click on the icon ticks done rather than expanding. The kind label and the rest of the heading still expand.
    - **`\event`** (`objects/eventKind.ts`, aliases `\ev` `\e` `\meet` `\meeting`): What / Date / Time (a range, "1-2pm") / Where. It reads meeting links as the place and birthday/travel cues. A start alone gets an hour (`endAfter`). Created through `buildCalendarEventInput`.
      - Its pane: the heading shows the time range, and editing the start keeps the event's length (`editEventWhen`).
      - Its body (`EventBody`): the place as a chip (📍, ↗ when it's a link, click to change), notes, links, Endeavour, the greyed options, the opt-in notification.
    - **Short weekdays in what's typed after the keyword** (`expandShortWeekdays`), only where they can only be a day:
      - before a time ("fri 1-2pm"), after on/next/this/by/until, or as the last word;
      - "sat down with Sam" keeps its verb.
      - The menu's own hint used "fri", which the shared parser didn't read.
  - **Fourth round (same day, the user's layout for the expanded pane):** one pane in four bands.
    - **Top bar** (the paragraph's own line): icon, kind, title, and of the options only **Important** (it matters most), then ↗ ▴.
    - **Second heading line** (`WhenLine.tsx`, first in the body, on the pane's tint, smaller): what's *on* and about the item. It holds the date and time (click-to-edit), Tentative ✏️ / Repeat 🔁, the state, then the kind's place 📍, Endeavour chip and notification chip.
    - **Content** (on the surface): notes and links.
    - **Bottom bar:** what's *off* or missing, greyed (+ Notes, + Link, + Place, + Endeavour, Important / Tentative / Repeat, Notify me).
    - **A click on an active option removes it** (❗ in the top bar, ✏️ 🔁 in the second line; hover tints it red and strikes it). It then reappears greyed in the bottom bar.
    - The collapsed (inline) pane is unchanged: its flag icons are part of the heading, so a click there still expands.
    - A trailing "important" / "urgent" / "asap" after `\` now comes off the title (`tidyObjectTitle`); Ctrl+Q prose keeps it. Seen in a screenshot.
    - Structure: `ArtifactBody` renders `WhenLine` and hands it to the kind's `Body` as `heading`, which `ObjectBody` puts first in its `subheading` band.
    - New `offExtra` slot for a kind's off things (`NotifyMeOption`). Active ones go in `extra` (`BeforeNotifyChip`, now null while off).
  - **Fifth round (same day, then Deadline):**
    - **Any empty space in the heading toggles the pane.** That covers the pieces' background (as before), the expanded box's own empty space (the plugin's `click` on the paragraph, keyed by `data-artifact-key`), and the second line's empty space (`onToggle` through `ArtifactBodyProps` → `ObjectBody`).
    - **The title highlights under the mouse** like the date and time (it's note text: a click edits it; the mark is no longer `cursor: pointer`).
    - **↗ and ▴ are right-aligned** in the expanded top bar (`paneActions`, floated right inside the box).
    - **Past and done are faded**, not struck through. The approach follows the usual practice: past events are faded in Google Calendar and Outlook; finished to-dos are faded rather than crossed out in Things; overdue unfinished to-dos stay prominent, with a warning colour, in Todoist and Things.
      - New state `past`: an event that has ended (a repeating one never is). `done`: ticked.
      - Both are drawn at reduced opacity, the whole box when expanded; hovering brings it back to full strength.
      - **overdue** (past, not done: reminders, deadlines, tasks) stays at full strength with "Overdue" in the warning colour.
      - The words "Done" and "Past" aren't shown: ☑ and the fading say it.
    - **`\deadline`** (`\dl`, `\due`, `\d`): built from the same definition as Reminder via `datedKindFactory.ts`. `reminderKind.ts` and `deadlineKind.ts` are one line each, so the two can't drift.
      - What, Due, By; tomorrow 12:00 when nothing is said.
      - Its pane is the reminder's: done in the icon slot, the options, the "1 day before, 17:00" setting chip.
      - Prerequisite fixed: `store/noteBacklinks.ts` now lists deadlines (and marks a done reminder or deadline as done), and the removed-link prompt names deadlines.
    - **Documentation:** CLAUDE.md "Inline objects in notes" was rewritten as one pattern with an explicit **"Adding a kind" checklist**.
    - **Test for future kinds:** a new test runs **every registered kind** end to end (`\` → created → linked → drawn → in Linked from), so a future kind can't skip a step the way deadlines nearly did.
  - **Sixth round (same day): repeating reminders, deadlines and events.**
    - **The pane follows the current occurrence** (`currentOccurrence`: the first from today on that isn't done). It shows that date, its ☐ ticks it (`doneDates`), and the pane moves on to the next, like a recurring to-do. Before this a repeating item showed its first date ever and had no done box.
      - A missed occurrence today reads Overdue.
      - ↗ opens the current occurrence.
    - **Its dates:** the date shows "▾" and drops down a list (`OccurrenceList`, `WhenLine.tsx`; data from `ArtifactTypeDef.occurrences`, `occurrenceWindow`): the rule ("Every week", `formatRepeatRule`), 3 before (faded), the current (NEXT), 8 after.
      - Each can be ticked (`toggleOccurrenceDone`) or opened alone in the calendar: `openArtifactTarget` gained `occurrenceDate`, and the calendar pane's existing "only this one / this and following" scope bar takes it from there. "Open the series ↗" sits at the bottom.
      - Clicking the date in the inline pane expands the pane with the list open (`occurrenceRequests.ts`).
      - Chosen over a hover reveal: a click works on touch, and holds still while you tick.
    - **Turning Repeat off asks first** ("Stop repeating?"; destructive confirm in the flag's `toggle`, so the second-line 🔁 and the right-click menu both ask). Reported by the user after losing a series to a stray click.
    - **Important moved before the title**, in both views.
    - **Bug found and fixed:** ticking the current date from the list closed the list. ProseMirror's `placeWidget` only matches the next existing widget, so the replaced heading widget made it build the body again.
      - Fix: `paneState.ts` (`usePaneState`, keyed per pane, cleared on collapse) holds the body's UI state (list open, the note/link/place drafts) so a rebuilt body resumes.
      - The initial value is stored too: the inline request can't be asked twice.
      - Tried first and dropped: handing ProseMirror the old DOM element back (it ended up detached in jsdom).
    - **Latent bug fixed on the way:** the expanded body had `overflow: hidden` (for its rounded corners), which would clip any dropdown in it, the Endeavour menu included. The bottom band rounds the corners now.
    - Tests: current occurrence and moving on, the list (window, tick, open one), inline date → list, ticking keeps the list open, repeat-off asks (cancel keeps, confirm removes), ❗ before the title. Seen in the app: list, tick, confirm.
    - Then built (seventh round, the user agreed): a date taken out of a series keeps its link back. See "Calendar: a date taken out of a repeating series stays linked" below.
  - **Icons, one per item kind, everywhere:** `config/itemIcons.ts` (`ITEM_TYPE_ICON`, `ITEM_FLAG_ICON`).
    - **Deadline is 🏁 (the finish line).** The user found ⏳ unclear. ⏳ already means a waiting task, 🚩 reads as "flagged", and ❗ is important. Deadlines had been ⏳, 🚩 and ⏰ in three places.
    - Used by the note panes, `NoteBacklinks`, `ListLinksBar`, `CalendarView` (the deadline type icon) and `overview/sources.ts`. As a result Overview's task ✔→☑️, reminder 🔔→⏰, list 📃→📋, list item 🔹→📃.
    - Pattern test.
  - **Date boxes** (`objects/whenInput.ts`, `readWhenInput`): the preview's fields and the pane's date/time.
    - These read short weekdays ("fri", "next sat"), which the shared parser deliberately doesn't: in prose "sat"/"wed" are words. A box holds only a date, so it's safe there.
  - **Files** (all under `components/NoteEditor/objects/` unless noted):
    - `types.ts`: the `NoteObjectKind<D>` contract.
    - `kinds.ts`: `NOTE_OBJECT_KINDS`, `matchKinds`, `interpretQuery`.
    - `reminderKind.ts`: the Reminder kind.
    - `session.ts`: the session plugin state and draft decorations.
    - `actions.ts`: `acceptKind`, `resolveDraft`, `commitSession`, `applyResolvedArtifactLink`, `insertObjectTrigger`, `unlinkArtifact`.
    - `NoteObjectTrigger.ts`: the extension and keys.
    - `NoteObjectMenu.tsx` + CSS: the menu.
    - `artifactTypes.ts`: `ARTIFACT_TYPES`, with `summarize` (+ `dateLabel`/`timeLabel`), `toggleDone`, `editWhen`, `menu`, `Body`.
    - `calendarItems.ts`: `datedItem`, `updateDated`.
    - `whenInput.ts` (`readWhenInput`, `expandShortWeekdays`, the defaults, `endAfter`).
    - `eventKind.ts`, `EventBody.tsx`, `flags.ts`, `NotifyChips.tsx` (third round); `WhenLine.tsx` (fourth); `datedKindFactory.ts`, `deadlineKind.ts` (fifth; `reminderKind.ts` now uses the factory); `occurrenceRequests.ts`, `paneState.ts` (sixth).
    - `artifactGroups.ts` + `ArtifactLinks.module.css`: grouping, the pane, the expanded box, the body widget, `paneLineKeys`, the stored-mark guard.
    - `ArtifactBody.tsx`, `ObjectBody.tsx` + `ObjectBody.module.css`, `CalendarItemBody.tsx`: the body.
    - `format.ts`.
    - `contextMenu.ts`: the `note-editor.artifact-link` provider.
    - New `config/itemIcons.ts`.
    - **Changed:**
      - `extensions/ArtifactLinkMark.ts`: the `display` attribute; target attributes also parsed from `data-artifact-*`.
      - `NoteEditor.tsx`: the extensions, the menu, `getObjectContext`, the `\` toolbar button; the pending-link effect calls `applyResolvedArtifactLink`; a plain click on linked text no longer opens it.
      - `NoteEditor.module.css`: draft styling; the mark itself is unstyled.
      - `store/uiStore.ts`: `PendingArtifactLink` (+ `replaceWithTitle`).
      - `config/labels.ts` (`LABELS.noteObjects`) and `config/hotkeys.ts` (`notes-object`).
    - **Removed in the second revision:** `ArtifactCard.tsx`, `CalendarItemDetails.tsx` and `ArtifactCard.module.css` (the separate card with a labelled field grid).
  - **Decisions:**
    - A custom ProseMirror plugin rather than `@tiptap/suggestion` (not installed). The session is re-read from the text on every transaction, so undo, IME composition and edits mid-draft all hold.
    - A session starts only when a `\` is *typed*.
    - A link stays a mark over the note's own text (one representation; search, encryption and link cleanup unchanged).
    - **The pane is visual, not an editing form** (the user's words): no field labels, nothing repeated between heading and body, flags in the right-click menu, everything else via ↗.
    - **Expanded = the paragraph as the box, with the body inside it**, so heading and body are one element sized to the wider of the two. Rejected: a separate card under the paragraph (the user disliked it), and a box around a separate widget (widths couldn't match).
    - **A link mid-sentence, expanded, boxes its whole paragraph.**
  - **Bugs found and how:**
    - The draft's keyword and body drew a seam (screenshot).
    - `.configure()` during render tripped `react-hooks/refs`.
    - **Reported by the user:** All options with nothing typed made an unlinked reminder (regression test).
    - Whole-day "today" shown as overdue (screenshot; test).
    - A NoteBacklinks line shift moved a pinned pattern-test exception.
    - Second revision:
      - An empty strip under the expanded body: ProseMirror's separator `<img>` is forced `display: inline !important` by its stylesheet, so it's hidden with `!important` (DOM inspection).
      - End in an expanded pane jumped to the next paragraph (screenshot); `paneLineKeys`, with a test.
      - Making the mark inclusive let Enter carry the link onto the next line (screenshot); the stored-mark guard, with a test.
      - The commit's stored marks were set before the mark step, which cleared them (test).
  - **Verified:**
    - `tsc -b`, `npm run build`, full Vitest suite (1039). `objects/objects.test.ts` (66 tests) covers:
      - creating: registry, query parsing, session rules, the keys, quick create (text → title), Undo restoring the typed words, Ctrl+Enter / All options with and without text, Ctrl+Q's selection untouched;
      - the pane: one pane per multi-paragraph link, flag icons, live state, whole-day overdue, expanded as one box with an unlabelled body, heading clicks (toggle / done), date/time click-to-edit, notes click-to-edit, the flags menu, End plus typing extending a title, Enter after a title staying plain;
      - `readWhenInput`.
      - fifth round: every kind end to end, `\deadline` (reading, defaults, its pane), `past` vs repeating, no "Done" word, empty-space clicks on the box and the second line, ↗ ▴ grouped;
      - fourth round: the four bands (date/time in the second line, not the top bar), Important removed from the top bar and Tentative from the second line coming back greyed at the bottom, the place in the second line, `tidyObjectTitle`;
      - third round: the defaults, `\event` (range, link as place, defaults, a start alone, length kept on edit, its body's place and opt-in notification), one flag list for body and menu, greyed options turning on (Repeat asking how often), the deadline's notify chip, hover → done in the icon slot, `expandShortWeekdays`.
    - Three pattern tests.
    - Third round in the real app (Playwright, light and dark): the two-kind menu, the event preview reading "fri 1-2pm" and a meeting link, a reminder defaulting to tomorrow 12:00, the hover checkbox, both panes expanded with greyed options.
    - Driven in the real app with Playwright, light and dark, with screenshots: expanded empty, notes + link added in place, time edited in the heading, right-click menu, an Endeavour created and assigned from the pane, title edited at both ends, a plain line after a title.
  - **Not verified:**
    - Android (the toolbar `\` button is the touch path; Gboard composition untested on a device; gap F11).
    - Changing an Endeavour that has a colour set (only the no-colour fallback was seen).
  - **Not built (BACKLOG.md "Inline objects in notes — next"):** other kinds; custom objects (lists, trackers, annotation tags); usage-ranked menu order; a `\task` kind; a `Body` and `editWhen` for tasks (their panes are read-only apart from done/open); keeping note text and item title in step; parser misses seen in testing.
- [x] **Calendar: a date taken out of a repeating series stays linked** (2026-10-06). The user asked whether an occurrence edited on its own should keep a link to its series. Best practice says yes: iCalendar (RFC 5545) uses the series' UID plus `RECURRENCE-ID`, Google uses `recurringEventId` + `originalStartTime`, Outlook keeps "exceptions" of the master.
  - **Before:** "Edit only this one" made a standalone copy with `crossAppRefs: []`, which also cut its link to the note it was made from.
  - **Data:**
    - `seriesId` + `seriesDate` on `CalendarEvent` / `CalendarReminder` / `CalendarDeadline` (and their create inputs, plus `doneDates`). `calendarStore` v16 backfills nulls.
    - Mappers `series_id` / `series_date`, migration `044_series_links.sql` (**pending**).
  - **Store:**
    - `detach…Occurrence` and `split…Series` set them and keep `crossAppRefs`.
    - A detach moves that date's done mark to the copy; a split moves the marks from the split date on.
    - The store test that encoded "a copy drops its cross-app refs" was rewritten to the new rule, with the reason.
  - **`services/calendarSeries.ts`:** `changedDatesOf`, `unlinkFromSeries`, `afterSeriesDeleted`. Deleting a series from its pane asks "Also delete the N dates changed separately?". "Keep them" unlinks them.
  - **UI:**
    - `SeriesLinkBar` (`components/RecurrenceScopeBar/`) in the three calendar panes: "Part of a repeating series — stands in for its …" / "Continues a repeating series from …" / "Was part of a series that has been deleted", with Open the series and Unlink.
    - The note pane's list of dates (`ArtifactOccurrences.items[].changedId`) shows a changed date at the date it replaces, labelled with its own date and a "changed" tag. It opens and ticks the separate item.
  - Copy: `LABELS.calendarSeries`. Pattern recorded in CLAUDE.md "Repeating series — a date taken out stays linked".
  - **Verified:** `tsc -b`, full Vitest suite. Tests: store detach/split (links, refs, done marks), `calendarSeries.test.ts` (order, unlink, delete-ask both ways, no question without changed dates), the note list's changed entry. **Not verified in the app by hand:** the calendar panes' bar and the delete question.

- [x] **Notification actions rebuilt: Got it, per-occurrence Snooze, Postpone moves, Archive per occurrence** (2026-10-06).
  - **Why:** the user reported that Done in the bell didn't stick. The audit (BACKLOG.md "Notification actions — audit 2026-10-06") found five causes:
    - Migration 043 wasn't run, so done marks never synced.
    - Done on a snoozed repeating card ticked the series' first date.
    - Archive only archived tasks.
    - Bell cards survived the item being done elsewhere.
    - A snooze silenced every date up to it.
  - Re-specced with the user; the full table is in `docs/android/05-notifications.md` "Notification actions", and the rule summary in CLAUDE.md "Notifications".
  - **Data:** `remindOccurrence` on events/reminders/deadlines (which occurrence a snooze is for) and `seenDates` on events/deadlines (acknowledged with Got it). `calendarStore` v17 with a cumulative migrate, `setOccurrenceSeen(kind, id, date)`, mappers, migration `045_notification_acks.sql` (**pending**). Detach/split carry `seenDates` with the dates they take.
  - **`services/notifications/actions.ts`** (rewritten):
    - `acknowledgeOccurrence` (Got it), `markOccurrenceDone`, `completeNotificationTask`.
    - `snoozeTime` and `snoozeNotificationTarget(kind, id, until, occurrence)`.
    - `postponeDate(preset, occurrence, today)` and `postponeOccurrence`: a repeating item is detached first through `soloOccurrence`, still linked to its series.
    - `archiveOccurrence(…, wholeSeries)`, which returns an Undo.
    - `openNotificationTarget(kind, id, occurrence)`.
  - **`plan.ts`:** a snooze applies only to its own occurrence; done or seen occurrences (and snoozes for them) never notify. `useNotificationChecker`'s `isStale` drops a card whose occurrence is done or seen.
  - **UI:**
    - `NotificationCenter`'s card: Got it (✕ is the same), Done (deadlines), Snooze panel (presets, plus "in N min/hours"), Postpone panel (Tomorrow / Next week, plus date and `TimeInput`), Archive (repeating → "Archive the whole series" checkbox, off by default), Open.
    - Clear all is device-only, and says so in its tooltip.
    - Android shade: "Got it" on all three kinds, with snoozes carrying the occurrence.
    - Copy: `LABELS.notifications.card`, `LABELS.notifications.actions.done = 'Got it'`.
  - **Decisions:**
    - Got it doesn't strike out events or deadlines (one still happens, the other is still due); only reminders.
    - Clear all changes no item (the user's call).
    - Postpone presets are hidden when they wouldn't move the item later.
  - **Verified:** `tsc -b`, full Vitest suite. Tests: `plan.test.ts` (per-occurrence snooze, seen dates and their snoozes), new `actions.test.ts` (Got it per kind, snooze occurrence, postpone presets, one-off/repeating/event-length moves, archive one date vs. series, Undo). **Not verified in the app by hand:** the card's panels and the Android shade buttons.

- [x] **Note blocks: Timeline (`\timeline`)** (2026-10-06). The user asked for `\` to make note-taking structures, not only linked app items, starting with a timeline and with UX and looks as the priority. They asked for one simple style now, with room for more later. The rule for new blocks is CLAUDE.md "Note blocks".
  - **What it is:** a vertical rail of entries. Each entry has a dot, a short **When** (free text in the accent colour: "1914", "Day 3", "c. 1200 BC", "Q3") and ordinary rich text below it (paragraphs, lists, quotes). The entry being edited fills its dot. "+ Add entry" sits under the last entry, where the next dot would go; it shows on hover or while writing in the timeline, and always on touch. Light and dark come from the colour tokens.
  - **Typing it:** `\timeline`, `\tl`, `\t` or `\history`, then Tab, Enter or a space. The `\` menu now has two headings, "Create and link" (reminder, event, deadline) and "Add to the note" (timeline).
  - **Keys:** Enter in a When goes to its text. Enter on an empty last line starts the next entry. Enter on an entry that's still empty leaves the timeline (or removes it, if that was its only entry). Backspace at the start of a body or a When steps back instead of merging the two; in an empty entry, it removes the entry. Delete at the end of a When does nothing.
  - **Files:** `components/NoteEditor/extensions/Timeline.ts` (nodes `timeline` with a `variant` attribute, `timelineItem`, `timelineWhen`; `insertTimeline`; `TimelineBehaviour` for keys and decorations), `objects/timelineBlock.ts`, `objects/blockKinds.ts` (`NOTE_BLOCK_KINDS`), `objects/types.ts` (`NoteBlockKind`, `PickableKind`, `isBlockKind`), `objects/kinds.ts` (`matchPickable`, `chosenKind`), `objects/NoteObjectTrigger.ts` (keyword + space), `objects/actions.ts` (`acceptKind` inserts a block), `objects/NoteObjectMenu.tsx` (group headings), styles in `NoteEditor.module.css` ("Timeline"), strings in `LABELS.noteBlocks` and `LABELS.noteObjects.groupItems`/`groupBlocks`.
  - **Decisions:**
    - Modelled on the vertical activity and changelog timelines of Linear, GitHub and Notion's templates: dot and rail on the left, the date as a small label, the content as normal text.
    - The When is free text, not a date picker: history and project notes use "c. 1200 BC", "Week 2" or "Q3" as often as real dates.
    - Styles are a `variant` attribute, so new styles never change stored content. Candidate styles are listed in BACKLOG.md "Note blocks". (A second style, horizontal, was added 2026-10-07; see "Timeline: sorted by date, and a horizontal style".)
    - ~~No sorting by date yet, for the same free-text reason.~~ Superseded 2026-10-07 by "Timeline: sorted by date, and a horizontal style": the user asked for date sorting after all, reading flexible formats.
    - Entry bodies allow paragraphs, lists and quotes, but not headings (they would enter the table of contents and numbering) or nested blocks.
  - **Search and previews** need nothing new: `noteContentToText` walks every node, so a timeline's text is searchable.
  - **Android:** works as-is. The toolbar's `\` button opens the menu (tap Timeline), Enter works on the soft keyboard, and "+ Add entry" is always shown on touch.
  - **Verified:** `tsc -b`, Vitest (`extensions/Timeline.test.ts`: insertion by keyword + space, by Enter, and after text; the full Enter/Backspace/Delete flow; JSON and HTML round trip; the placeholder and the Add entry button. New pattern test: every `*Block.ts` is registered). Seen in the real app with Playwright, light and dark: the menu groups and a four-entry timeline.
  - **Not verified:** on an Android device; drag-and-drop of whole entries (the browser's default only).

- [x] **Timeline: sorted by date, and a horizontal style** (2026-10-07). The user asked for date sorting after all ("it is a timeline"), reading flexible formats (a full date, just a year, "January 1990"…), and for a second style that runs horizontally with entries alternating above and below the line, "something delighting". Rules: CLAUDE.md "Note blocks".
  - **Reading a When** (`utils/timelineWhen.ts`, pure, `readTimelineWhen`):
    - Full dates: `01/01/1990` (day or month first from the locale when both could be either, via `isDayFirstLocale`, now exported from `utils/textToTask.ts`), `1990-01-01`, `1.2.90`, `28 June 1914`, `1st of January, 1990`, `January 1, 1990`.
    - Partial: `1990`, `January 1990`, `Jan '90`, `1990-03`, `28 June`, `June`, `Q3 2024` / `2024 Q3`, `H2 2024`, `Summer 1969`.
    - Eras and spans: `c. 1200 BC`, `44 B.C.`, `AD 79`, `33 CE`, `the 1990s`, `late '60s`, `1800s` (a century), `19th century`, and ranges (`1914–1918`, `1914-18`, `June – July 1914`, `500–400 BC`), which read as their start, taking the year and era from the end.
    - Times: `9:30am`, `14:05`, `noon`, alone or with a date.
    - Counted steps: `Day 3`, `Week 12: wrap-up`, `Phase 2` (their own scale).
    - Not a when: plain text, or a short number with no era (`Room 79`).
  - **Ordering** (`readTimeline`):
    - Entries are read on the scale most of them use; a When without a year takes the year of the dated entry before it (shown greyed after the When, "23 July · 1914"), and a time alone takes its date.
    - An entry that can't be placed stays right after the entry it follows, so notes under an undated heading move with it; undated entries before the first dated one stay first.
    - Stable: equal dates keep their typed order.
  - **When it sorts:** when the cursor leaves an entry (`sortOnLeave`, `appendTransaction`), never while typing in it. The entry the cursor goes into is pinned at its index, so a new entry stays where it was made instead of jumping along with the one it followed. The entry that moved flashes in its new place (`data-tl-moved`, 1.6 s, plugin state mapped through edits; off under reduced motion). Order is a timeline attribute, `order`: `asc` (default) / `desc` / `manual`.
  - **Horizontal style** (`variant: 'horizontal'`): a two-row grid of half-card columns. Entry n spans columns n and n+1, top row when odd and bottom when even, so neighbours overlap by half a card on opposite sides and the dots sit evenly along the line. Each card draws its own stretch of the line, its stem and its dot in the gap between the rows (`::after` / `::before`); the stretches overlap into one line. The When is a pill at the top of the card. Scrolls sideways when it's wider than the note. "+ Add entry" is a dashed dot at the end of the line, pinned to the column after the last card (`--tl-end`), since the rows can differ in height.
  - **Choosing:** a small pill at the timeline's top right (shown on hover, while editing in it, always on touch): the two styles as line drawings, and the order ("↓ Oldest first" → "↑ Newest first" → "As typed"). The same choices in the right-click menu (`extensions/timelineMenu.ts`, Style ▸ / Order ▸). Both go through `setTimelineSettings`, which sorts at once.
  - **Fixed along the way:** Enter on an empty entry left the timeline only if it was the last one; with sorting a new entry needn't be last, so any empty entry leaves now (found in the real app: the next paragraph was typed into the timeline).
  - **Decisions:** sorting on leave rather than live (live reordering under the cursor is disorienting; Notion/Craft-style databases sort only on commit); pinned new entry; the inferred year is shown rather than silently used, so a wrong guess is visible.
  - **Verified:** Vitest (`utils/timelineWhen.test.ts`, 10 cases across formats and ordering; `extensions/Timeline.test.ts`, now 13: sort on leave + flash + pinned new entry, never while typing or "as typed", newest first from the button, inferred year chip, an empty middle entry leaving, horizontal columns and sides). Seen in the real app with Playwright, light and dark: entries typed out of order sorted, both styles, the current card.
  - **Not verified:** on Android (the pill is always shown on touch; the horizontal style scrolls with a finger).

- [x] **Ctrl+= / Ctrl+− change a heading's level** (2026-10-07). On a heading, Ctrl+= makes it more prominent (H3 → H2) and Ctrl+− less (H2 → H3, H5 → normal text); H1 stays H1. Anywhere else they still zoom. `extensions/HeadingLevel.ts` (`shiftHeadingLevel`) takes the keys in the editor's keymap; `App.tsx`'s zoom handler now skips a key that was already handled (`e.defaultPrevented`), since the editor sees it first. Tested in `HeadingLevel.test.ts`.

- [x] **The Glossary: Definitions and Concepts as records, and text linked to them** (2026-10-07). The user asked to "highlight text and apply an existing definition or concept to it — like the text highlight, and perhaps a large bracket pointing at the concept", and to build out the Definition and Concept tags. Choices confirmed with the user: records like Acronym; one Glossary across all notes.
  - **Records:** Definition (`meaning`) and Concept (`summary`) are now structured tag types (`config/structuredTagTypes.ts`), so applying them (Ctrl+3 / Ctrl+2, the # Tag picker, or `\` on a selection) opens the existing create popover and makes a `StructuredTagEntry`, like Acronym. The popover is prefilled by `utils/definitionInference.ts`: a whole definition splits into term and meaning ("X is / means / refers to Y", "X: Y", "X – Y"); a selected term finds its meaning in the sentence around it; otherwise the meaning is left empty rather than guessed. `GLOSSARY_TYPES` / `glossaryMeaning` say which field holds each type's meaning (Acronyms are in the Glossary too).
  - **Linking text to a term:** select text, type `\` (or the toolbar's `\` button): the selection menu (`objects/SelectionMenu.tsx`, state in `objects/selectionMenuState.ts`) offers Glossary terms, suggested from the selected words first (`rankSuggestions`, the "link a …" picker pattern), then Mark as: Definition…, Concept…, Important / Very important / Critical, Review later. Typing filters both. The text gets a `conceptRef` mark (`extensions/ConceptRef.ts`: `entryId`, `typeKey`).
  - **How linked text looks:** a soft highlight in the term's colour with a dotted underline; beside it, in the right margin, a brace spanning the text's lines and pointing at a label pill (icon + term). Hover the label for a card with the meaning and where it's defined; click it to go there. Hovering either side highlights the other. Labels that would overlap stack downwards with a curved leader from the brace's tip. The editor makes room only while the note has linked text (`data-concept-margin`: `wide` = 15rem, or `compact` = icon-only labels in a narrow editor). Drawn by a plugin view in a layer beside the editor (measured from `coordsAtPos`, corrected for the editor's CSS zoom; redrawn on doc change, resize, and store changes), never per mark element: a mark is split per line, so per-element drawing would repeat.
  - **Glossary view** (`components/NotesSection/GlossaryView.tsx`, from the # button's panel; `uiStore.notesGlossaryOpen`): every Definition, Concept and Acronym A–Z with sticky letters and an A–Z jump strip, search (term, meaning, note), kind filter chips, and per term: meaning, "Defined in <note>" (opens it at the definition) and "N references" (expands to the notes referring to it; each opens at the reference). Model in `services/glossary.ts` (`glossaryEntries`, `glossaryEntry`, `glossaryReferences` via `countConceptRefs`). Encrypted notes' entries show while unlocked and as locked otherwise.
  - **Opening a note at a passage** (new, shared by the Glossary, labels, Key points and Review): `services/notePassage.ts` `openNotePassage(noteId, { mark, attr, value })` finds the tab holding the passage, opens the note there and sets `uiStore.requestedNotePassage`; NoteEditor selects the first run of that mark and scrolls it to the middle.
  - **Right-click** on linked text: Go to where it's defined / Remove link (`extensions/annotationMenu.ts`).
  - **Bugs found and fixed while testing in the real app:** a `\` over a selection spanning paragraphs replaced the text (ProseMirror doesn't route that through `handleTextInput`), so the key is also caught on keydown; and keys typed quickly after `\` (before the menu's search box had focus) went into the editor and replaced the selection, so while the menu is open the editor forwards keys to it (`selectionMenuKey` in the trigger's storage).
  - **Verified:** Vitest (`extensions/annotations.test.ts`: linking across bold, unlinking, the Glossary list and references, `\` on a selection within and across paragraphs, the toolbar button; `objects/SelectionMenu.test.tsx`: suggestion first + Enter links, keys forwarded before focus, filtering to Critical; `utils/definitionInference.test.ts`). Seen in the real app with Playwright: the definition popover prefilled, the menu's suggestion, braces/labels/hover card, the Glossary.
  - **Not built:** see BACKLOG.md "Glossary and Important — follow-ups" (existing plain Definition/Concept marks aren't turned into records; one term defined in two notes stays two entries; Quick Access search for terms).

- [x] **Important, built out: levels, margin markers, Key points, Review later** (2026-10-07). The user chose all three proposals for "build out Important".
  - **Levels:** Important ⭐, Very important 🌟, Critical ❗ (`extensions/importanceLevels.ts`; accent colours amber/orange/red; Critical also reads bold). Ctrl+1 now steps Important → Very important → Critical → off (it used to toggle on/off). Also from `\` on a selection and the right-click menu.
  - **Where it all lives:** on the Important tag's own mark (`NoteTagMark`: `level`, `passageId`, `reviewDue`, `reviewStep`, rendered as `data-*`), so it syncs and encrypts with the note and needs no store, migration or Supabase column. Logic in `extensions/Importance.ts` (`collectImportantPassages`, `setImportance` keeps the passage id and schedule, `cycleImportance`, `toggleReview`, `nextReview`).
  - **Margin markers:** one per line block, the highest level's icon in the left margin (plus 🔁 when on the review list). The inline ⭐ before Important text was dropped in favour of it.
  - **Key points:** the outline pane (`NoteTOC`) lists the note's Important passages, most important first, with the review date; click to go to the passage.
  - **Review later:** puts a passage on a review list, first due tomorrow. The Review view (`components/NotesSection/ReviewView.tsx`, from the # button's panel, which shows the due count; `uiStore.notesReviewOpen`) shows one passage at a time: its text, the paragraph around it with the passage picked out, and the note (click to open it there). "Got it" pushes the next review out (1, 3, 7, 16, 35, 90 days), "Again" brings it back tomorrow, "Stop reviewing" takes it off. A segmented bar shows progress, coloured by answer. The queue is taken when the view opens, so answering never reshuffles it. `services/noteReview.ts` (`reviewItems`, `dueReviews`, `answerReview`) reads passages from every note's stored content (`utils/noteContent.ts` `collectReviewPassages`) and writes the new schedule back with `updatePassageInContent`, or through the editor when that text is open there (`services/liveNoteEditor.ts`, registered by NoteEditor), so the editor's next save can't write over it.
  - **Decisions:** a fixed interval ladder rather than SM-2 ease factors (two buttons, no grading; "light" was the ask); scheduling in the mark rather than a store (no migration, travels with copy-paste and sync); review only for Important passages (Review later makes a passage Important if it wasn't).
  - **Verified:** Vitest (`extensions/annotations.test.ts`: level cycle keeping one passage id, one marker per line at the highest level, review scheduling and the interval ladder, JSON/HTML round trip, due passages with context, answering through the store and through a live editor, other passages untouched). Seen in the real app with Playwright, with the clock moved a day on: the Review card.
  - **Not verified:** on Android (the Review buttons are plain taps; there's no hover dependency).

- [x] **`objects.test.ts` no longer depends on today's date** (2026-10-07). Six tests parsed "tomorrow" against a fixed 6 Oct but drew labels against the real clock, so they failed from 7 Oct. `Date` is now faked to the test's `NOW` (`vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ['Date'] })`; real timers are still needed by `flush()`).

- [x] **Note blocks: Quote (`\quote`)** (2026-10-07). The user asked for a quote block with "a big quotation mark and the quote in italics", a choice of designs, fields for the quote, who said it, when and where ("and anything else"), and fields that can be hidden. Rules: CLAUDE.md "Note blocks".
  - **Making one:** `\quote` (or `\q`, `\cite`) then a space, Tab or Enter; or select text, type `\` and choose Turn into ▸ Quote (the words become the quote, the cursor goes to Who).
  - **Fields** (`QUOTE_FIELDS`): Who, Role, Source, When, Where, Link; Who, Source and When show by default. They read as one attribution line: "— Albert Einstein · The Saturday Evening Post · 1929". Each field is a small block of its own (`quoteField`), laid out inline. An empty field shows, with its placeholder, only while the quote is being edited. The first field shown leads with a dash, the rest follow a dot (decorations `data-qb-lead` / `data-qb-sep` / `data-qb-hide`).
  - **Hiding fields:** the field chips in the pill, or right-click ▸ Show. A hidden field leaves the document, and what it said is kept in the quote's `hidden` attribute, so showing it again brings the text back in its place.
  - **Styles** (`variant`):
    - **Classic:** a large accent “ in the margin, the words in italic serif.
    - **Card:** a soft card with an accent edge, a small “, and the speaker's initials in a circle before their name (`initials()`, first and last word).
    - **Pull quote:** centred, large semi-bold serif between two accent rules, with the attribution in spaced capitals.
    Chosen from the pill at the top right (line drawings of each) or right-click ▸ Style.
  - **Keys:** Enter goes from the quote to each field in turn and out after the last; Tab / Shift+Tab move between parts; Backspace at a part's start steps back; Shift+Enter is a line break in the quote; Enter on an empty quote turns it back into a paragraph.
  - **Decisions:**
    - The style is chosen per quote (the pill), not in Settings as the user first floated. It's faster to change where you're looking, and it matches the timeline. A "default style for new quotes" setting is in BACKLOG.md if wanted.
    - Fields are blocks rather than attributes, so their text is searchable and copyable like everything else.
    - The two blocks' pills now share one look through `data-block-tools`.
  - **Bug found in the real app:** an empty field broke the attribution onto a new line (ProseMirror's placeholder `<br>` in an empty block); hidden in empty fields.
  - **Files:** `extensions/Quote.ts`, `extensions/quoteMenu.ts`, `objects/quoteBlock.ts`, `objects/SelectionMenu.tsx` (Turn into), `NoteEditor.module.css` ("Quote"), `LABELS.noteBlocks.quote`.
  - **Verified:** Vitest (`extensions/Quote.test.ts`: `\quote` and the Enter flow, Tab/Shift+Tab/Backspace, hide/show keeping text, empty fields while editing, style + JSON/HTML round trip + Quote from a selection, initials). Seen in the real app with Playwright, light and dark: all three styles, the pill, a field shown and typed.

- [x] **Subtitle and Author paragraph styles** (2026-10-07). Ctrl+H, S and Ctrl+H, A (also in the style dropdown and right-click ▸ Style). `extensions/NoteBylines.ts`: own nodes like the Title, so never numbered and never in the outline; Enter continues in ordinary text. Design from editorial convention:
  - **Subtitle** (standfirst): 1.3rem, regular weight, muted, tucked under the title.
  - **Author** (byline): small spaced capitals, semi-bold, after a short accent rule. Distinct from the subtitle in size, case and weight.
  - **Bug avoided:** with Ctrl still held, Ctrl+A is the editor's Select all, which runs before the Ctrl+H handler sees it; the handler puts back the selection from when Ctrl+H was pressed (`headingSelectionRef`). Tested through the mounted editor (`NoteEditor.test.tsx`).

- [x] **F2 renames the open tab** (2026-10-07). F2 in the editor opens the tab's name for editing; Enter or Esc puts the cursor back exactly where it was (`startRenameActiveTab` / `returnFromRename`, `renameReturnRef`); clicking away doesn't. Tested in `NoteEditor.test.tsx`.

- [x] **Font size** (2026-10-07). Ctrl+Shift+> / Ctrl+Shift+< (code Period / Comma) step the selection's size up or down a ladder (10 … 48 px, `extensions/fontSize.ts`, Tiptap's `FontSize` on the textStyle mark), starting from the block's own size (a heading starts from its size, not 16). The ribbon has A− [size] A+; the size box shows the size in use and offers Default. Tested in `extensions/fontSize.test.ts`.

- [x] **Style ribbon tidied** (2026-10-07). The text-colour button is now the same hue-wheel swatch as the selection toolbar's, from the new shared `components/Icons` (`TextColorIcon`, with the colour in use in its middle). This started the suite-wide pattern "Icons come from one place" (CLAUDE.md), and the ItemActions icons moved there too. The `\` button shows only on Android, where it's the touch path for `\` (gap F11); with a keyboard, typing `\` is quicker.

- [x] **Copying out of a note keeps its structure as plain text** (2026-10-07). The user pasted Notes text elsewhere and lost every heading, bullet and number. Rich targets were already fine (they take the HTML); plain-text targets got bare lines. `noteClipboardText.ts` (ProseMirror's `clipboardTextSerializer`) now writes Markdown-style text: `#` headings and Title, `-` bullets and `1.` numbers (nested, honouring a list's start), `>` quotes (a quote block with "— attribution"), fenced code, `---`, tables as tab-separated rows (they paste into a spreadsheet as cells), timelines as "- When — what", and links as "text (url)". A few words copied from inside one block come out as just the words. Tested in `noteClipboardText.test.ts`.

- [x] **"Normal text" keeps Glossary links** (2026-10-07). `normalText.ts` keeps `conceptRef` with the other meaningful marks (links, tags, linked items).

- [x] **Note blocks: Cycle (`\cycle`), and Block designs named as a suite pattern** (2026-10-07). The user asked for a cycle block (stages leading one to the next and back to the first, at least two; "the global water cycle"), with several designs like the quote and timeline, reversible and two-way arrows, "other cool features", and for the multiple-designs approach to become a named global pattern. Also asked: how hard are icons and pictures in blocks? (Answer: an icon slot was quick and is built; free-form picture resizing is backlogged — BACKLOG.md "Pictures and icons in note blocks".)
  - **Block designs** (CLAUDE.md "Block designs", BACKLOG.md "Block designs — how to develop them"): the shared half is `extensions/blockDesigns.ts` (`BlockDesign`, `blockPill`, `designButtons`, `designMenuItem`, `pillButton`, `openBlockPopover`). The timeline (`TIMELINE_DESIGNS`) and quote (`QUOTE_DESIGNS`) were moved onto it; their right-click Style ▸ is now Design ▸. The pattern test checks every block uses it. `hooks/useEscapeClose.ts` gained `registerEscapeClose` for overlays that aren't React components.
  - **The cycle:** a name, then stages (`cycleStage`: a label and a short note). Each stage owns the arrow to the next; the last one's returns to the first.
    - **Designs:** **Ring** (stages round a circle, the name in the middle, arrows as arcs between them, their names outside the ring); **Flow** (a row of cards with arrows between and the return loop underneath; scrolls sideways when long); **Steps** (a list down the page, arrows between and the loop back as a bracket on the left).
    - **Arrows:** click one (or right-click a stage ▸ This arrow) for a popover: direction Forward / Both ways / Backward, and a name for the step ("Evaporation"). Reverse the cycle (pill ⇄, or right-click) turns every arrow round.
    - **Options** (pill and right-click): numbered stages (badges in the stage's colour); colours One colour / Spectrum (each stage a hue round the wheel); + Add stage.
    - **Icons:** each stage has an icon slot (empty slots show while hovering or editing). Click it for an emoji grid, any typed emoji, "Choose a picture…", or Remove; or drop a picture on it. Pictures are centre-cropped to a 160 px square (`utils/imageCompress.ts` `squareIconDataUrl`). Shape (circle / rounded / square) and size (S / M / L) apply to the whole cycle.
    - **Keys:** Enter goes name → stage → its note → the next stage (the next one if it's still empty, else a new one); Enter in an empty stage leaves the cycle (removing it while more than two would remain); Tab / Shift+Tab between stages; Backspace steps back, removing an empty stage only while more than two remain.
    - **Copy as text:** the name, "- Stage — note" lines, each arrow with its name, and "(back to <first>)".
  - **Files:** `extensions/Cycle.ts`, `extensions/cycleMenu.ts`, `objects/cycleBlock.ts`, `extensions/blockDesigns.ts` + `BlockDesigns.module.css`, `NoteEditor.module.css` ("Cycle"), `noteClipboardText.ts`, `LABELS.noteBlocks.cycle` / `.design`.
  - **Decisions:**
    - Arrows belong to the stage they leave, so adding, removing or reordering stages keeps each arrow with its stage.
    - Ring positions and colours are computed in decorations (CSS variables), never stored.
    - The Ring's arcs are drawn from its geometry (the ring is square), not measured from the DOM, so they never lag the layout.
  - **Bugs found in the real app:**
    - The three starter stages were skipped: Enter from a note always inserted a new stage. It now moves into an empty next stage.
    - Flow arrow names were clipped: an explicit `min-width` replaced the grid's content minimum.
    - The Flow pill was clipped by its own sideways scroll.
  - **Verified:** Vitest (`extensions/Cycle.test.ts`, 7: the Enter flow, leaving and the two-stage minimum, arrow direction/name and reverse, settings + icon round-trip through JSON and HTML, widgets per stage and ring placement, copy as text; the Block designs pattern test). Seen in the real app with Playwright, light and dark: a four-stage water cycle with named arrows, icons, Spectrum and numbering in all three designs, and the arrow popover.
  - **Not verified:** dropping a real picture (the file input and drop path are wired, but only emoji were tried in the browser); on Android (gap F15).

- [x] **Note blocks: select a whole block; cycle stages can be deleted; ring arrows meet their cards** (2026-10-07). Three requests after trying the cycle.
  - **Select a whole block** (every note block, through Block designs): the Select button at the end of each pill, Select whole block in the right-click menu, and Esc while the cursor is inside a block (`NoteBlockSelect` in `extensions/blockDesigns.ts`, with `NOTE_BLOCK_NODES`, `blockAround`, `selectBlock`, `blockPosAt`). The block becomes a ProseMirror NodeSelection, so Ctrl+C / Ctrl+X / Delete act on all of it, and its plain-text copy is the whole block (`noteClipboardText`). It's outlined, with a soft tint. Block roots render `data-note-block`.
    - **Bugs found in the real app:** the outline rule never matched, because the CSS module renamed `.ProseMirror-selectednode` (now `:global(...)`); and the text selection toolbar appeared for a selected block (`FloatingToolbar` now ignores NodeSelections, which also covers a selected image).
  - **Delete a stage:** a × in each stage's corner (on hover, faintly while the cycle is being edited, always on touch), and Delete this stage in the right-click menu; both stop at two stages (`deleteStage`, `canDeleteStage`).
  - **Ring arrows meet their cards:** the arcs used to stop a fixed distance from each stage's centre, so a card without an icon (shorter) was left with a gap. Now `RingLayout` (the cycle plugin's view) measures every card after each render and on resize, and `ringArcEnds` walks along the circle from each card's centre until it leaves the card's box, so every arc ends at the card's edge whatever it holds. The first draw uses default sizes until measured.
  - **Verified:** Vitest (`extensions/blockDesigns.test.ts`: Esc selects each kind of block, a second Esc passes on, Delete removes the block, its copy is the block; deleting stages down to two; arc ends move with card size; the pattern test now requires Select on every block). Seen in the real app with Playwright: the ring without icons with arrows touching, the stage ×, the selected-block outline.

- [x] **Breakdown block, block frames, and linking to the Glossary as you type** (2026-10-07).
  - **Breakdown (`\breakdown`, also `\parts`, `\pillars`, `\components`):** the user asked for "a concept made up of other things" (the pillars of democracy) with several designs, and for a name; "Breakdown" was chosen as the general one, and Pillars is one of its designs. A whole (`breakdownWhole`) and at least two parts (`breakdownPart`: a label and a short note), in `extensions/Breakdown.ts`.
    - **Designs:**
      - **Hub:** the whole in a circle at the centre, parts around it on dashed spokes. Spokes run from the centre to each part under the cards, so they always meet both.
      - **Pillars:** the whole as a temple's pediment over a row of fluted columns, with capitals and plinths, on a stylobate.
      - **Tree:** the whole on top, an org-chart branch line down to the parts.
    - **Options:** Spectrum colours; + Add part; a × per part (down to two, also in the right-click menu). Keys as the cycle's. Copies as "Democracy, made up of:" plus a list.
    - Files: `extensions/Breakdown.ts`, `breakdownMenu.ts`, `objects/breakdownBlock.ts`, CSS "Breakdown".
  - **Frames on every block:** the user asked for "outline the block" and "background shade the block", with elegant defaults only. Outline and Shade toggles in every pill and in Frame ▸ in every right-click menu (`blockFrameAttributes`, `frameButtons`, `frameMenuItem`, `toggleBlockFrame` in `extensions/blockDesigns.ts`). Drawn outside the block's box (an outline at an offset and a spread shadow), so no design's layout moves; the margin grows to make room; the pill sits on the frame's edge (inside, in the designs that scroll sideways). The timeline, quote and cycle gained the attributes too.
  - **Linking to the Glossary as you type** (`extensions/GlossaryAutolink.ts`): the user's workflow (define "eukaryote" in note X; type it in note Y; get offered a link back; accept with Enter or Tab, or keep typing; Backspace or Ctrl+Z straight after undoes it).
    - When the word just typed (or its plural) is a Glossary term, it's underlined and a chip follows it: "↵ Link to 📖 Eukaryote" (the meaning on hover). Enter or Tab links it; the link is its own undo step (`closeHistory`), so Ctrl+Z takes the link off and keeps the typing. Backspace straight after removes the link (not the text). Esc, or typing on past it, dismisses it.
    - **The design question, answered:** offered **once per note per term**. Not again in a note that already links the term (the convention of linking a term's first mention, so a page isn't a sea of links), and not again in a note where the offer was passed over (Esc, typing on, or the Backspace undo) in this session. A different note gets its own offer.
    - Never offered in code, inside an existing link, in the term's own defining passage, or during a `\` session. Longest term first, so a multi-word term beats a shorter one inside it.
    - **Hovering linked text** now shows the term's card under the word (what it means, where it's defined, "Go to where it's defined ↗") — in addition to the margin label's card (`ConceptMarginView` in `extensions/ConceptRef.ts`; `cardContent` is shared by both).
    - The user's "future enhancement" (definitions linking to definitions) partly comes for free: typing a term inside another term's definition gets the same offer. The rest is in BACKLOG.md "Glossary auto-linking — follow-ups".
  - **Charts:** not built. The user wanted the libraries' trade-offs first (BACKLOG.md "Charts in notes").
  - **Verified:**
    - Vitest: `extensions/Breakdown.test.ts`, 7 tests — the Enter flow and `\pillars` alias, deleting parts down to two, spokes, design round-trip, and frames on every block kind (toggle, pill state, HTML round trip). `extensions/GlossaryAutolink.test.ts`, 7 tests — offer and Enter, plural and Tab, mid-word silence and going away, Backspace undo and no re-offer, Ctrl+Z, once per note across notes, not in its own definition. The pattern test now requires frames on every block.
    - Seen in the real app with Playwright: the three designs with four parts, Spectrum + outline + shade, the offer chip, and the hover card.
  - **Not verified:** Android (gaps F15, F16).
- [x] **Recurring tasks** (2026-10-07).
  - **What and why:** a task can repeat (every N days/weeks/months/years; ending never, after N times or on a date). Asked for 2026-10-01 and designed with the user 2026-10-07: the next occurrence appears **when this one is completed** (one open at a time, like most to-do apps, rather than a list made in advance), and it carries the title, notes, Endeavour, tags, priority, its sub-tasks (reset to undone), its links to notes and lists, and a link back to the occurrence it came from.
  - **Data:** `Task.repeat: RepeatConfig | null` (taskStore v14; migration `046_task_repeat.sql`, mapper `repeat`); new task-link kind `repeatOf` (🔁, "Repeat of" / "Repeated as", doesn't block) in `config/itemLinkKinds.ts`.
  - **Files:** `services/recurringTasks.ts` (all the rules: `nextOccurrenceDate`, `spawnNextOccurrence`, `nextOccurrenceOf`, `discardOccurrence`, `repeatSummary`); `services/taskListLinks.ts` (`toggleTaskWithLists` spawns after completing and returns the new id); `services/taskCompletion.ts` (the Completed toast adds "Next one: <date>", and Undo deletes it for good); `agent/access.ts` (`toggleTask` spawns too, so the agent and the UI agree); `components/RepeatField/` (the editor), used in `TaskPane` (above Priority, hidden for sub-tasks) and `AddTaskModal` (top of More options); `TaskItem` shows 🔁 with the rule as its tooltip. Labels: `LABELS.recurring`.
  - **Decisions:** the next date is the first on the rule after this occurrence's deadline (else scheduled date, else today) that isn't in the past, so a weekly task completed three weeks late doesn't leave three overdue copies; the scheduled date moves by the same amount, and times are kept. Setting a repeat on a task with no date sets its deadline to today (a repeat needs somewhere to start). Sub-tasks can't repeat themselves (the parent's sub-tasks are copied instead). Completing, reopening and completing again reuses the occurrence already made. Rejected: making the next occurrence on a schedule (the calendar's way), since the user wanted one open at a time.
  - **Verified:** `services/recurringTasks.test.ts`, 8 tests — next date (interval, month overflow, completed late, until/count ends), the occurrence's carried fields, reset sub-tasks, `repeatOf` link and calendar deadline, no duplicate on re-completing, the count running down, the toast and its Undo (nothing left in the Recycling Bin), and the agent path. `mappers.test.ts` round-trips `repeat`. Seen in the real app with Playwright: the editor in the pane (deadline set to today), and completing it (the next occurrence appears, toast "Next one: 14 Oct 2026"). **Not verified:** Android (gap F17).
  - **Not built:** see BACKLOG.md "Recurring tasks" (weekday rules, skip, reopen-without-Undo).
- [x] **Definitions and concepts: "go to" lands on the definition, and the location names the notebook** (2026-10-07).
  - **What and why:** the user reported that going to where a term is defined scrolled to the bottom of the note, and that the location showed only the note's name.
  - **Bug found and how:** reproduced with Playwright (definition in the middle of a long note, Glossary → "Defined in…"): the passage was selected and scrolled to, then opening the note focused the editor at the end a frame later (Tiptap's delayed `focus('end')`), which moved both cursor and scroll to the bottom. `NoteEditor`'s `requestedNotePassage` effect now selects two frames on (not cancelled when the request is cleared, which re-runs the effect at once) and scrolls the passage to a fifth of the way down the view (`showNearTop`), rather than the browser's centre.
  - **Location:** `GlossaryEntry.notebookPath` (`services/glossary.ts`, from the new `utils/notes.ts` `getNoteNotebookPath`); the Glossary card and the term's hover card say "Defined in <note> · <notebook>", and hovering it shows the whole path (`LABELS.glossary.definedAt`, "University › Biology › Cells › <note>").
  - **Verified:** in the real app with Playwright (the definition ends up ~150 px below the top of the editor, its text selected); `utils/notes.test.ts` covers `getNoteNotebookPath`. Not covered by a test: the scroll itself (needs layout).
  - **Not built:** a hierarchy of linked concepts (BACKLOG.md "Glossary auto-linking — follow-ups").
- [x] **Note pictures: resize like a word processor, and crop** (2026-10-07).
  - **What and why:** the user asked for pictures to resize by dragging corners and edges, as in Word, instead of the one small square at the right, and for cropping dragged the same way.
  - **How:** `extensions/ResizableImage.ts` is now a node-view class. Selected, a picture shows eight handles. A corner keeps the shape, following whichever way the pointer moved further; an edge stretches one way, after which the picture keeps its own `height`. A bar on the picture offers **Crop** and **Original size** (back to the picture's own size and shape, keeping the crop). Crop shows the whole picture with the cut-away parts dimmed and L-shaped crop handles (each side moves on its own; at least 5% stays). Enter, Done or a click elsewhere applies; Esc (the Escape stack, `registerEscapeClose`) or Cancel leaves it; "Show all" resets. Applying keeps the scale, so the picture's new width is what's left of the whole one.
  - **Data:** new image attributes `height` (only once stretched) and `crop` (`{ top, right, bottom, left }` fractions; `data-crop="t,r,b,l"` in HTML). Nothing is cut out of the image itself, so a crop can be widened again later. No migration (note content is JSON).
  - **Files:** `extensions/ResizableImage.ts`, `extensions/imageGeometry.ts` (pure: `resizeDrag`, `cropDrag`, `parseCrop`/`serializeCrop`), `extensions/ResizableImage.module.css` (it also beats the editor's old `.editorContent img` margin), labels `LABELS.noteImage`. Pointer events with capture, so touch works too.
  - **Bugs found and how:** with Playwright: the drag measured the outer frame, taller than the picture, so stretching sideways also changed the height (now measures the picture); the editor's `.editorContent img` margin pushed the picture 12 px down inside its frame.
  - **Verified:** `extensions/imageGeometry.test.ts`, 6 tests (corner keeps shape, edges stretch, minimum size, crop sides, crop minimum, attribute round-trip); in the real app with Playwright: corner, edge, Original size, crop (left edge and a corner), Enter applies, Ctrl+Z restores. **Not verified:** Android (gap F19). Copied HTML carries width and height but not the crop (BACKLOG.md).
- [x] **Selecting text marks its other mentions** (2026-10-07).
  - **What and why:** the user asked that selecting text "shadow-highlights" the other places the term comes up in the note, as code editors do.
  - **How:** `extensions/OccurrenceHighlight.ts`, decorations only. A selection of 2–100 characters within one block marks every other match in the open tab, case-insensitively. Whole words match only whole words when the selection is whole words (nothing of a word on either side); otherwise anywhere ("itos" finds "mitosis"). Capped at 500. The colour is the new `--color-occurrence` token (a faint yellow, lighter in dark mode).
